\Xi_{\mu}(s,x,r,\mu)=-\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)-\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\rho(s,x,r,\mu),

(7.6)

where

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\rho(s,x,r,\mu)={(\hbox{det\,}[a(r,\mu)])_{\mu}\over 2\hbox{det\,}[a(r,\mu)]}+{\langle[a(r,\mu)^{-1}]_{\mu}(x-\mu),(x-\mu)\rangle\over 4(r-s)},\\
&\langle[a(r,\mu)^{-1}]_{\mu}(x-\mu),(x-\mu)\rangle=\begin{pmatrix}\langle[a(r,\mu)^{-1}]_{\mu_{1}}(x-\mu),(x-\mu)\rangle\\
\langle[a(r,z)^{-1}]_{\mu_{2}}(x-\mu),(x-\mu)\rangle\\
\vdots\\
\langle[a(r,z)^{-1}]_{\mu_{n}}(x-\mu),(x-\mu)\rangle\end{pmatrix}.\end{aligned}\right.

(7.7)

Moreover, under assumption
(H4)
, it is easy to check that

\left\{\begin{aligned} &|\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)|\leqslant K{1\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}},\\
&|\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)|\leqslant K{1\over(r-s)^{{n+1\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}},\\
&|\rho(s,x,r,\mu)|\leqslant K\left(1+{|x-\mu|^{2}\over(r-s)}\right),\end{aligned}\right.

(7.8)

for some
0<\lambda<\lambda_{0}
. In what follows, we denote

\tilde{\varphi}(s,x;\theta,\theta^{0}):=\tilde{\varphi}\big(s,\,x,\,\theta(s,x),\,\theta_{x}(s,x),\,\theta^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta(s,x)),\,\theta_{x}^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta(s,x)),\,\theta_{y}^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta(s,x))\big),

for
\varphi(\cdot)=\tilde{b}(\cdot),\tilde{g}(\cdot)
, and

\displaystyle\tilde{g}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})

\displaystyle:=\tilde{g}^{0}\big(t,\,s,\,\tilde{x},\,x,\,\theta(s,x),\,\theta_{x}(s,x),\,\theta^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta(s,x)),\,\theta_{x}^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta(s,x)),

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\theta_{y}^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta(s,x)),\,\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)\big).

(7.9)

First, we establish a
C^{0,1}
-norm estimate for
(\Theta(\cdot,\cdot),\Theta^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},\cdot,y))
.

Lemma 7.2
.

There exists a constant
\kappa>0
, independent of
\theta(\cdot)
and
\theta^{0}(\cdot)
, such that

\displaystyle\|\Theta(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}+\sup_{t,\tilde{x},y\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}}\|\Theta^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},\cdot,y))\|_{C^{0,1}}

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\kappa\Big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1}}+\sup_{t,\tilde{x},y\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}}\|h^{0}(t,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{C^{1}}\Big].

(7.10)

Proof.

By (
7.3
) and (
7.6
), using the method of integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle\Theta_{x}(s,x)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big[\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)h_{x}(\mu)-\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)\rho(s,x,T,\mu)h(\mu)\big]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})\Big]d\mu dr.

(7.11)

Then from the estimate (
7.8
), we get

\displaystyle|\Theta_{x}(s,x)|

\displaystyle\leqslant\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}K{1\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\Big[|h_{x}(\mu)|+\Big(1+{|x-\mu|^{2}\over(r-s)}\Big)|h(\mu)|\Big]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}K{1\over(r-s)^{{n+1\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}(1+|\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)|)d\mu dr

\displaystyle\leqslant K(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1}})+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}K{1\over(r-s)^{{n+1\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}|\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)|d\mu dr.

By Grönwall’s inequality, we obtain

|\Theta_{x}(s,x)|\leqslant K(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1}}),\quad\forall(s,x)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}.

Substituting the above into (
7.3
) and then by (
7.8
) again, we have

|\Theta(s,x)|\leqslant K(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1}}),\quad\forall(s,x)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}.

It follows that

\|\Theta(\cdot\,,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}\leqslant K(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1}}).

(7.12)

Similar to (
7.11
), we get

\displaystyle\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big[\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)h^{0}_{x}(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)-\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)\rho(s,x,T,\mu)h^{0}(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\Big]d\mu dr.

(7.13)

Note that
(t,\tilde{x},y)
serve only as parameters in (
7.4
).
By the same argument as the above, we get

\sup_{t,\tilde{x},y\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}}\|\Theta^{0}(t,\cdot\,,\tilde{x},\cdot\,,y))\|_{C^{0,1}}\leqslant K\sup_{t,\tilde{x},y\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}}\big[1+\|h^{0}(t,\tilde{x},\cdot\,,y)\|_{C^{1}}\big].

Combining the above with (
7.12
), the desired estimate (
7.10
) is obtained.
∎

The following gives the regularity estimate of

\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)
with respect to the parameters
t
,
\tilde{x}
and
y
.

Lemma 7.3
.

There exists a constant
\kappa>0
, independent of
\theta(\cdot)
and
\theta^{0}(\cdot)
, such that

\|\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},0,\alpha,1,2}}\leqslant\kappa\big[1+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|^{2}_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1,2}}\big].

(7.14)

Proof.

From (
7.4
) and (
7.13
), it is easily seen that
both
\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)
and
\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)
are differentiable with respect to the parameter
y
.
Moreover, the derivatives are given by

\displaystyle\Theta_{y}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)h^{0}_{y}(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big[\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big[\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)h^{0}_{xy}(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)-\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)\rho(s,x,T,\mu)h^{0}_{y}(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big[\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\big]d\mu dr.

Applying the arguments employed in the proof of
7.2
, we have

\|\Theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\Theta^{0}_{xy}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leqslant K\big(1+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1,1}}\big).

(7.15)

By continuing the above argument, we get

\displaystyle\Theta_{yy}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)h^{0}_{yy}(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)d\mu+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big\{\Theta_{xyy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big[\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\big\langle\tilde{g}_{p^{0}p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big\rangle\Big\}d\mu dr.

\displaystyle\Theta_{xyy}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big[\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)h^{0}_{xyy}(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)-\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)\rho(s,x,T,\mu)h^{0}_{yy}(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big\{\Theta_{xyy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big[\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\big\langle\tilde{g}_{p^{0}p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big\rangle\Big\}d\mu dr.

Note from (
7.15
) that

\big|\big\langle\tilde{g}_{p^{0}p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big\rangle\big|\leqslant K\big(1+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|^{2}_{C^{0,0,1,1}}\big).

Then by the arguments employed in the proof of
7.2
again, we get

\|\Theta^{0}_{yy}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\Theta^{0}_{xyy}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leqslant K\big(1+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|^{2}_{C^{0,0,1,2}}\big).

(7.16)

For any
\tilde{x}_{1},\tilde{x}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
, denote

\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,x,y)=\Theta^{0,1}(t,s,x,y)-\Theta^{0,2}(t,s,x,y)\quad\hbox{with}\quad\Theta^{0,i}(t,s,x,y)=\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x}_{i},x,y),\quad i=1,2.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)[h^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-h^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,1})-\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,2})\Big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle\delta\Theta_{y}^{0}(t,s,x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)[h_{y}^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-h_{y}^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\Big\{\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\mu,y)\big[\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,1})\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\Theta^{0,2}_{xy}(t,r,\mu,y)\big[\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,1})-\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,2})\big]\Big\}d\mu dr,

and

\displaystyle\delta\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,s,x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)[h_{x}^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-h_{x}^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)\rho(s,x,T,\mu)[h^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-h^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,1})-\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,2})\Big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle\delta\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,s,x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)[h_{xy}^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-h_{xy}^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)\rho(s,x,T,\mu)[h_{y}^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-h_{y}^{0}(t,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big\{\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\mu,y)\big[\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,1})\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\Theta^{0,2}_{xy}(t,r,\mu,y)\big[\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,1})-\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y;\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0,2})\big]\Big\}d\mu dr.

Using the estimates (
7.8
) and (
7.15
), we get

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{1\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0,0}}d\mu|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{1\over(r-s)^{n\over 2}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\Big[|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\mu,y)|\Big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta_{y}^{0}(t,s,x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{1\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0,1}}d\mu|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{1\over(r-s)^{n\over 2}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\Big\{|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\mu,y)|

\displaystyle\quad+\big[|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\mu,y)|\big]|\Theta^{0,2}_{xy}(t,r,\mu,y)|\Big\}d\mu dr,

and

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,s,x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{1\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\Big[\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,1,0}}+\Big(1+{|x-\mu|^{2}\over(r-s)}\Big)\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0,0}}\Big]d\mu|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{1\over(r-s)^{n+1\over 2}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\Big[|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\mu,y)|\Big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,s,x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{1\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\Big[\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,1,1}}+\Big(1+{|x-\mu|^{2}\over(r-s)}\Big)\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0,1}}\Big]d\mu|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{1\over(r-s)^{n+1\over 2}}e^{{-\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\Big\{|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\mu,y)|

\displaystyle\quad+\big[|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\mu,y)|\big]|\Theta^{0,2}_{xy}(t,r,\mu,y)|\Big\}d\mu dr.

Note from (
7.15
) that
\Theta^{0,2}_{xy}(\cdot)
is globally bounded,
and the estimate of
\|\Theta^{0,2}_{xy}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}
is independent of
(\theta(\cdot),\theta^{0}(\cdot))
.
Thus, by the definition of the seminorm
\|\cdot\|_{\alpha}
and the Grönwall’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{(t,s,x,y)}\left[\|\Theta^{0}(t,s,\cdot\,,x,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,s,\cdot\,,x,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta_{y}^{0}(t,s,\cdot\,,x,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,s,\cdot\,,x,y)\|_{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leqslant K\big(1+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,1,1}}\big).

By continuing the above arguments, we can also have

\displaystyle\sup_{(t,s,x,y)}\big[\|\Theta_{yy}^{0}(t,s,\cdot\,,x,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta_{xyy}^{0}(t,s,\cdot\,,x,y)\|_{\alpha}\big]\leqslant K\big(1+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|^{2}_{C^{0,\alpha,1,2}}\big).

Similarly, for the parameter
t
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{(s,\tilde{x},x,y)}\Big[\|\Theta^{0}(\cdot\,,s,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\Theta_{x}^{0}(\cdot,s,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\Theta_{y}^{0}(\cdot,s,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\Theta_{xy}^{0}(\cdot\,,s,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\|\Theta_{yy}^{0}(\cdot\,,s,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\Theta_{xyy}^{0}(\cdot\,,s,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}\Big]\leqslant K\big(1+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},0,1,2}}\big).

Combining the above together,
we get the estimate (
7.14
) immediately.
∎

Remark 7.4
.

Note that (
7.14
) is a global prior estimate and the constant
\kappa>0
is independent of the choice of

(\theta(\cdot),\theta^{0}(\cdot))
. Thus, we could always assume that

\|\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}+\|\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},0,\alpha,1,2}}\leqslant\kappa\big[1+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|^{2}_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1,2}}\big],

(7.17)

where
\kappa>0
is same as that given in
7.3
.

Next, we are going to establish the
C^{1+\alpha}
-norm estimate for
\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)
and
\Theta(s,\cdot)
.
To achieve this, we need to make some preparations.
By making the transforms
x-\mu=(\sqrt{T-s})\tilde{\mu}
and
x-\mu=(\sqrt{r-s})\tilde{\mu}

in the first integral term and the second integral term of (
7.11
)–(
7.13
),
respectively, we have

\displaystyle\Theta_{x}(s,x)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}(s,x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})\big[h_{x}(x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})-\rho(s,x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})h(x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})\big]d\tilde{\mu}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\Big[\Theta_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})\Big]d\tilde{\mu}dr,

(7.18)

and

\displaystyle\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}(s,x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})\Big[h^{0}_{x}(t,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu},y)

\displaystyle\quad-\rho(s,x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})h^{0}(t,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu},y)\Big]d\tilde{\mu}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\Big[\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},y)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\Big]d\tilde{\mu}dr,

(7.19)

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Xi}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})

\displaystyle:=-\Xi(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})(r-s)^{n\over 2}

\displaystyle=-{1\over(4\pi)^{n\over 2}(\hbox{det\,}[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})])^{1\over 2}}e^{-{\langle a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})^{-1}\tilde{\mu},\tilde{\mu}\rangle\over 4}},

\displaystyle\rho(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})

\displaystyle={(\hbox{det\,}[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})])_{\mu}\over 2\hbox{det\,}[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})]}+{\langle[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})^{-1}]_{\mu}\tilde{\mu},\,\tilde{\mu}\rangle\over 4},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})

\displaystyle:=-\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})(r-s)^{n\over 2}

\displaystyle={1\over(4\pi)^{n\over 2}(\hbox{det\,}[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})])^{1\over 2}}e^{-{\langle a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})^{-1}\tilde{\mu},\tilde{\mu}\rangle\over 4}}{a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})^{-1}\over 2\sqrt{r-s}}\tilde{\mu}.

(7.20)

By some straightforward calculations, it is clearly seen that

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\Xi}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})|\leqslant Ke^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}},\quad

\displaystyle|\rho(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})|\leqslant K(1+|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}),

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})|\leqslant{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}e^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}},\quad

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\Xi}(s,\cdot,r,\cdot-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\|_{\alpha}\leqslant Ke^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\rho(s,\cdot,r,\cdot-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\|_{\alpha}\leqslant K(1+|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}),\quad

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,\cdot,r,\cdot-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\|_{\alpha}\leqslant{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}e^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}},

(7.21)

for some
0<\lambda<\lambda_{0}
.

Proposition 7.5
.

There exist two constants
0<\varepsilon\leqslant T
and
\bar{\kappa}>0
such for any
\theta(\cdot)\in C^{{\alpha\over 2},1+\alpha}

and
\theta^{0}(\cdot)\in C^{{\alpha\over 2},{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1+\alpha,2}
with

\|\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\leqslant 2\bar{\kappa}\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big],

(7.22)

the unique solution
(\Theta(\cdot),\Theta^{0}(\cdot))
of PDE (
7.1
) satisfies

\displaystyle\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\Theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\leqslant 2\bar{\kappa}\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big].

(7.23)

Moreover, there exists a constant
\widehat{\kappa}>0
, which depends on
\bar{\kappa}
, such that

\displaystyle\|\Theta^{0}_{xy}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\leqslant\widehat{\kappa}\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,1}}\big].

(7.24)

Proof.

For any
x_{1},\,x_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
, from (
7.18
) and then by the estimate (
7.21
), we have

\displaystyle|\Theta_{x}(s,x_{1})-\Theta_{x}(s,x_{2})|

\displaystyle\leqslant\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}Ke^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}}(1+|\tilde{\mu}|^{2})d\tilde{\mu}\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1}}|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}e^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}}d\tilde{\mu}dr\big[1+\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\big]|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}Ke^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}}\big|h_{x}(x_{1}-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})-h_{x}(x_{2}-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})\big|d\tilde{\mu}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}Ke^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}}(1+|\tilde{\mu}|^{2})\big|h(x_{1}-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})-h(x_{2}-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})\big|d\tilde{\mu}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}e^{-\lambda|\tilde{\mu}|^{2}}\Big[\big|\Theta_{x}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})-\Theta_{x}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\big|\tilde{b}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})-\tilde{b}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})\big|\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\big|\tilde{g}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})-\tilde{g}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})\big|\Big]d\tilde{\mu}dr.

(7.25)

Note that for
\tilde{\varphi}(\cdot)=\tilde{b}(\cdot),\tilde{g}(\cdot)
,

\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})-\tilde{\varphi}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\quad=\tilde{\varphi}\Big(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},\theta(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu}),\theta_{x}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu}),

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,\theta^{0}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},\theta(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})),

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,\theta_{x}^{0}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},\theta(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})),

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,\theta_{y}^{0}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},\theta(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu}))\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad-\tilde{\varphi}\Big(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},\theta(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu}),\theta_{x}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu}),

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,\theta^{0}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},\theta(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})),

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,\theta_{x}^{0}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},\theta(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})),

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,\theta_{y}^{0}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},\theta(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu}))\Big).

Then by
7.3
(or (
7.17
)), we get

\displaystyle|\tilde{\varphi}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})-\tilde{\varphi}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\big(1+\|\theta_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{C^{\alpha}}+\|\theta^{0}_{x}(r,\cdot,r,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0}}\big)|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha},

where
K>0
depends on
h(\cdot)
and
h^{0}(\cdot)
.
Substituting the above into (
7.25
) and then by
7.2
, we have

\displaystyle|\Theta_{x}(s,x_{1})-\Theta_{x}(s,x_{2})|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\big(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}\big)|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}\Big[\|\Theta_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\theta_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{C^{\alpha}}+\|\theta^{0}_{x}(r,\cdot,r,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0}}\Big]dr|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha},

which implies that

\|\Theta_{x}(s,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}\leqslant K\big(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}\big)+\int_{s}^{T}{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}\Big[\|\Theta_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\theta_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{C^{\alpha}}+\|\theta^{0}_{x}(r,\cdot,r,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0}}\Big]dr.

(7.26)

By the same argument as the above (noting (
7.19
)), we also have

\displaystyle\|\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leqslant K\big(1+\|h^{0}(t,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}\Big[\|\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\theta_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\theta^{0}_{x}(r,\cdot,r,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0}}\Big]dr,

(7.27)

\displaystyle\|\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,s,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leqslant K\big(1+\|h^{0}(t,\tilde{x},\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha,1}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}\Big[\|\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,s,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\theta_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\theta^{0}_{x}(r,\cdot,r,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha,0}}\Big]dr.

(7.28)

Combining (
7.26
) and (
7.27
) yields that

\displaystyle\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\Theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\bar{\kappa}\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big]+\bar{\kappa}\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big[\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}([T-\varepsilon,T])}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\Theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\big],

(7.29)

where
\bar{\kappa}>\kappa
, only depending on
(h(\cdot),\tilde{g}(\cdot),h^{0}(\cdot),\tilde{g}^{0}(\cdot))
, is a fixed constant.
Let
\varepsilon
be small enough such that
\bar{\kappa}\sqrt{\varepsilon}\leqslant{1\over 4}
and

\|\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\leqslant 2\bar{\kappa}\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big].

(7.30)

Then from (
7.29
), we get

\displaystyle\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\Theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\leqslant 2\bar{\kappa}\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big].

Substituting (
7.30
) into (
7.28
) also yields (
7.24
) immediately.
The proof is complete.
∎

The following gives the
C^{\alpha\over 2}
-norm estimate for
\Theta^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)
and
\Theta(\cdot,x)
.

Proposition 7.6
.

There exists a constant
\check{\kappa}>0
such for any
\theta(\cdot)\in C^{{\alpha\over 2},1+\alpha}

and
\theta^{0}(\cdot)\in C^{{\alpha\over 2},{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1+\alpha,2}
satisfying (
7.22
),
the unique solution of PDE (
7.1
) satisfies

\displaystyle\|\Theta(\cdot,x)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot,x)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\Theta^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\Theta_{y}^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}

\displaystyle+\|\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}\,\leqslant\,\check{\kappa}\big(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big),\quad\forall(t,\tilde{x},x,y)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R},

(7.31)

on the time interval
[T-\varepsilon,T]
.

Proof.

For any
T-\varepsilon\leqslant s_{1}\leqslant s_{2}\leqslant T
, by (
7.18
) we have

\displaystyle|\Theta_{x}(s_{1},x)-\Theta_{x}(s_{2},x)|

\displaystyle\leqslant\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Big[|\tilde{\Xi}(s_{1},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})|\|h_{x}(\cdot)\|_{\alpha}|\tilde{\mu}|^{\alpha}|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}

\displaystyle\quad+\big|\tilde{\Xi}(s_{1},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})-\tilde{\Xi}(s_{2},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{2}}\tilde{\mu})\big|\|h_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad+|\tilde{\Xi}(s_{1},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})\rho(s_{1},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})|\|h(\cdot)\|_{\alpha}|\tilde{\mu}|^{\alpha}|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}

\displaystyle\quad+\big|\tilde{\Xi}(s_{1},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})\rho(s_{1},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})

\displaystyle\qquad-\tilde{\Xi}(s_{2},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{2}}\tilde{\mu})\rho(s_{2},x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s_{2}}\tilde{\mu})\big|\|h(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big]d\tilde{\mu}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}|\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{1},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})|\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot)\tilde{b}(\cdot;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}\big(\cdot;\theta,\theta^{0}\big)\|_{L^{\infty}}d\tilde{\mu}dr

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s_{2}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big|\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{1},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})-\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{2},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\tilde{\mu})\big|

\displaystyle\qquad\times\|\Theta_{x}(\cdot)\tilde{b}(\cdot;\theta,\theta^{0})+\tilde{g}\big(\cdot;\theta,\theta^{0}\big)\|_{L^{\infty}}d\tilde{\mu}dr

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s_{2}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}|\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{1},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})|\Big[\big|\Theta_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\qquad-\Theta_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\tilde{\mu})\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})\big|+\big|\tilde{g}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\qquad-\tilde{g}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})\big|\Big]d\tilde{\mu}dr.

(7.32)

Note that (recalling (
7.20
))

\displaystyle\int_{s_{2}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big|\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{1},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\tilde{\mu})-\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{2},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\tilde{\mu})\big|d\tilde{\mu}dr

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}+K\int_{s_{2}}^{T}\Big|{1\over\sqrt{r-s_{2}}}-{1\over\sqrt{r-s_{1}}}\Big|dr\leqslant K|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}.

Then by (
7.17
),
7.5
and (
7.21
),
from (
7.32
) we obtain

\displaystyle|\Theta_{x}(s_{1},x)-\Theta_{x}(s_{2},x)|\leqslant K\big(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big)|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2},

\displaystyle\forall T-\varepsilon\leqslant s_{1}\leqslant s_{2}\leqslant T,\,x\in\mathbb{R}^{n},

(7.33)

where
K>0
depends on
\bar{\kappa}
.
Recall (
7.19
) and note that

\displaystyle\Theta^{0}_{y}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}(s,x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})h^{0}_{y}(t,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu},y)d\tilde{\mu}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\Big[\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},y)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},y)\Big]d\tilde{\mu}dr,

and

\displaystyle\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}(s,x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu}\Big[h^{0}_{xy}(t,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu},y)

\displaystyle\quad-\rho(s,x,T,x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu})h^{0}_{y}(t,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{T-s}\tilde{\mu},y)\Big]d\tilde{\mu}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu})\Big[\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},y)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}_{p^{0}}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu};\theta,\theta^{0},\Theta^{0})\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\tilde{\mu},y)\Big]d\tilde{\mu}dr.

By the same argument as the above, we also have

\displaystyle|\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s_{1},\tilde{x},x,y)-\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s_{2},\tilde{x},x,y)|\leqslant K\big(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big)|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2},

\displaystyle|\Theta^{0}_{y}(t,s_{1},\tilde{x},x,y)-\Theta^{0}_{y}(t,s_{2},\tilde{x},x,y)|+|\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,s_{1},\tilde{x},x,y)-\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,s_{2},\tilde{x},x,y)|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\big(1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,1}}\big)|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\forall T-\varepsilon\leqslant s_{1}\leqslant s_{2}\leqslant T,\,(t,y,\tilde{x},x)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{n}.

(7.34)

The rest can be obtained easily.
∎

Remark 7.7
.

Note that the constant
\check{\kappa}>0
in
7.6
is independent of
the
{\alpha\over 2}
-Hölder norm of
(\theta(\cdot,\cdot),\theta^{0}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot,\cdot,\cdot))
with respect to the second argument
s
.
We assume that

\displaystyle\|\theta(\cdot,x)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\theta_{x}(\cdot,x)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\theta^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\theta_{x}^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}+\|\theta_{y}^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}

\displaystyle+\|\theta_{xy}^{0}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}\leqslant\check{\kappa}\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big],

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\forall(t,\tilde{x},x,y)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R},

(7.35)

on the time interval
[T-\varepsilon,T]
.

The following is concerned with the local solvability of the equilibrium HJB equation (
2.25
).

Proposition 7.8
.

There exists a constant
\bar{\varepsilon}\in(0,\varepsilon]
such that the equilibrium HJB equation (
2.25
)
admits a unique classical solution on the time interval
[T-\bar{\varepsilon},T]
.

Proof.

Denote

{\cal B}_{\varepsilon}=\big\{(\theta(\cdot),\theta^{0}(\cdot))\in C^{{\alpha\over 2},1+\alpha}\times C^{{\alpha\over 2},{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1+\alpha,2}~\big|~(\theta(\cdot),\theta^{0}(\cdot))\hbox{ satisfies }\eqref{lem:v-par-estimate},\eqref{Prop:Holder1}\hbox{ and }\eqref{Prop:Holder-t-v}\big\}.

For any
(\theta(\cdot),\theta^{0}(\cdot))\in{\cal B}_{\varepsilon}
, by
7.1
,
PDE (
7.1
) admits a unique classical solution
(\Theta(\cdot),\Theta^{0}(\cdot))
.
Moreover, from
7.2
,
7.3
,
7.5
and
7.6
,
we know that
(\Theta(\cdot),\Theta^{0}(\cdot))\in{\cal B}_{\varepsilon}
.
Thus, the mapping
\Gamma(\cdot,\cdot):{\cal B}_{\varepsilon}\to{\cal B}_{\varepsilon}
, given by

\Gamma(\theta(\cdot),\theta^{0}(\cdot))=(\Theta(\cdot),\Theta^{0}(\cdot)),

is well-defined. For any
(\theta^{i}(\cdot),\theta^{0,i}(\cdot))\in{\cal B}_{\varepsilon}
(
i=1,2
), let

(\Theta^{i}(\cdot),\Theta^{0,i}(\cdot))=\Gamma(\theta^{i}(\cdot),\theta^{0,i}(\cdot)),\quad i=1,2.

Denote

\displaystyle\delta\theta(s,x)=\theta^{1}(s,x)-\theta^{2}(s,x),\quad\delta\theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)=\theta^{0,1}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)-\theta^{0,2}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y),

\displaystyle\delta\Theta(s,x)=\Theta^{1}(s,x)-\Theta^{2}(s,x),\quad\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)=\Theta^{0,1}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)-\Theta^{0,2}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y),

\displaystyle\delta\tilde{\varphi}(s,x)=\tilde{\varphi}(s,x;\theta^{1},\theta^{0,1})-\tilde{\varphi}(s,x;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2}),\quad\hbox{for}\quad\varphi(\cdot)=b(\cdot),g(\cdot),

\displaystyle\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)=\tilde{g}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y;\theta^{1},\theta^{0,1},\Theta^{0,1})-\tilde{g}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y;\theta^{1},\theta^{0,2},\Theta^{0,2}).

We hope to show that

\|\delta\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},1+\alpha}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}\leqslant{1\over 2}\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},1+\alpha}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}\big],

(7.36)

on some time interval
[T-\bar{\varepsilon},T]\subseteq[T-\varepsilon,T]
.
Thus,
\Gamma(\cdot)
is a contraction mapping and then it admits a unique fixed point

(\Theta(\cdot),\Theta^{0}(\cdot))\in C^{{\alpha\over 2},1+\alpha}\times C^{{\alpha\over 2},{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1+\alpha,2}
.
Then we get the well-posedness of the following equation:

\displaystyle\Theta(s,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)h(\mu)d\mu+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)\tilde{b}\big(r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu),\Theta_{x}(r,\mu),

\displaystyle\quad\,\,\Theta^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),\Theta_{x}^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),\Theta_{y}^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu))\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}\big(r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu),\Theta_{x}(r,\mu),\Theta^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),\Theta_{x}^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),\Theta_{y}^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu))\big)\Big]d\mu dr;

\displaystyle\Theta^{0}(t,s.\tilde{x},x,y)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,T,\mu)L(t,\tilde{x},\mu,y)d\mu+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\tilde{b}\big(r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu),\Theta_{x}(r,\mu),\Theta^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),\Theta_{x}^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),

\displaystyle\quad\,\,\Theta_{y}^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu))\big)+\tilde{g}^{0}\big(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,\Theta(r,\mu),\Theta_{x}(r,\mu),\Theta^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),

\displaystyle\quad\,\,\Theta_{x}^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),\Theta_{y}^{0}(r,\mu,r,\mu,\Theta(r,\mu)),\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\big)\Big]d\mu dr.

By
7.1
,
(\Theta(\cdot),\Theta^{0}(\cdot))
is the unique classical solution of
equilibrium HJB equation (
2.25
) on
[T-\bar{\varepsilon},T]
.

In the following, let us show that (
7.36
) really holds for some
\bar{\varepsilon}>0
.

Step 1.

From (
7.3
)–(
7.4
), we have

\displaystyle\delta\Theta(s,x)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta^{1}_{x}(r,\mu)\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)+\delta\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})+\delta\tilde{g}(r,\mu)\Big]d\mu dr,

(7.37)

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta_{x}^{0,1}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)+\Theta_{x}^{0,1}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Big]d\mu dr.

(7.38)

By
7.2
and the estimate (
7.8
), from (
7.37
) we get

|\delta\Theta(s,x)|\leqslant K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}\Big[|\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)|+|\delta\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)|+|\delta\tilde{g}(r,\mu)|\Big]d\mu dr.

(7.39)

For
\varphi(\cdot)=b(\cdot),g(\cdot)
, by
7.3
(or (
7.17
)) we have

\displaystyle|\delta\tilde{\varphi}(s,x)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\Big[|\delta\theta(s,x)|+|\delta\theta_{x}(s,x)|+|\theta^{0,1}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))-\theta^{0,2}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{2}(s,x))|

\displaystyle\quad+|\theta_{x}^{0,1}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))-\theta_{x}^{0,2}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{2}(s,x))|

\displaystyle\quad+|\theta_{y}^{0,1}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))-\theta_{y}^{0,2}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{2}(s,x))|\Big]

\displaystyle\leqslant K\Big[|\delta\theta(s,x)|+|\delta\theta_{x}(s,x)|+|\delta\theta^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))|

\displaystyle\quad+|\delta\theta_{x}^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))|+|\delta\theta_{y}^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))|\Big].

(7.40)

Substituting the above into (
7.39
) yields that

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta(s,x)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K(T-s)\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}|\delta\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)|d\mu dr.

(7.41)

Similar to (
7.40
), using
7.3
(or (
7.17
)) again, we have

\displaystyle|\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\Big[|\delta\theta(s,x)|+|\delta\theta_{x}(s,x)|+|\delta\theta^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))|+|\delta\theta_{x}^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))|

\displaystyle\quad+|\delta\theta_{y}^{0}(s,x,s,x,\theta^{1}(s,x))|+\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)\Big].

(7.42)

Substituting the above into (
7.38
), we get

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K(T-s)\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|d\mu dr.

(7.43)

From (
7.11
) and (
7.13
), we have

\displaystyle\delta\Theta_{x}(s,x)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta^{1}_{x}(r,\mu)\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)+\delta\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})+\delta\tilde{g}(r,\mu)\Big]d\mu dr,

(7.44)

\displaystyle\delta\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta_{x}^{0,1}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)+\Theta_{x}^{0,1}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Big]d\mu dr.

(7.45)

By
7.2
and the estimates (
7.8
), (
7.40
) and (
7.42
), we get

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta_{x}(s,x)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\sqrt{T-s}\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{n+1\over 2}}|\delta\Theta_{x}(r,\mu)|d\mu dr,

(7.46)

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\sqrt{T-s}\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{n+1\over 2}}|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|d\mu dr.

(7.47)

Combining (
7.41
), (
7.43
), (
7.46
) and (
7.47
) together,
and then by the Grönwall’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\big].

(7.48)

Step 2.

From the proof of
7.3
, by some direct computations, it is easily seen that

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}_{y}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})+\delta\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta^{1},\theta^{0,1},\Theta^{0,1})\delta\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})+\delta\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta^{1},\theta^{0,1},\Theta^{0,1})\delta\Theta_{xy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Big]d\mu dr,

and

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}_{yy}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta^{0,1}_{xyy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\Theta^{0}_{xyy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})+\delta\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Theta^{0,1}_{xyy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta^{1},\theta^{0,1},\Theta^{0,1})\delta\Theta_{xyy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\delta\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2},\Theta^{0,2})\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2},\Theta^{0,2})\Theta^{0,2}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\rangle\Big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}_{xyy}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big[\Theta^{0,1}_{xyy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\Theta^{0}_{xyy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})+\delta\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Theta^{0,1}_{xyy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta^{1},\theta^{0,1},\Theta^{0,1})\delta\Theta_{xyy}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\delta\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2},\Theta^{0,2})\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\Theta^{0,1}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\tilde{g}^{0}_{p^{0}p^{0}}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2},\Theta^{0,2})\Theta^{0,2}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y),\,\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)\rangle\Big]d\mu dr.

Then by
7.3
and the estimate (
7.8
), we get

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta^{0}_{y}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K(T-s)\Big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}\Big[|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|\Big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\sqrt{T-s}\Big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{{n+1\over 2}}}\Big[|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|\Big]d\mu dr,

and

\displaystyle|\delta\Theta^{0}_{yy}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K(T-s)\Big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{{n\over 2}}}\Big[|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xyy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|\Big]d\mu dr,

\displaystyle|\delta V_{xyy}(t,y,z,s,x)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\sqrt{T-s}\Big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{{n+1\over 2}}}\Big[|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{xyy}(t,r,\tilde{x},\mu,y)|\Big]d\mu dr.

Combining the above with the estimate (
7.48
) together, by Grönwall’s inequality again we get

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,,0,1,2}([T-\varepsilon,T])}

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\sqrt{\varepsilon}K\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\big].

For any
\tilde{x}_{1},\tilde{x}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
, denote

\delta\widehat{\Theta}^{0}(t,s,x,y)=\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x}_{1},x,y)-\delta\Theta^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x}_{2},x,y).

Then from (
7.45
), we have

\displaystyle\delta\widehat{\Theta}_{x}^{0}(t,s,x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Xi_{x}(s,x,r,\mu)\Big\{[\Theta_{x}^{0,1}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-\Theta_{x}^{0,1}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)]\delta\tilde{b}(r,\mu)

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\widehat{\Theta}^{0}_{x}(t,r,\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})+\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)\Big\}d\mu dr.

Note that

\displaystyle|\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)-\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{2},\mu,y)|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K|\delta\widehat{\Theta}_{x}^{0}(t,s,x,y)|+K|\delta\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,s,\tilde{x}_{1},x,y)|\big[1+\|\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,s,\cdot,x,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta^{0,2}_{x}(t,s,\cdot,x,y)\|_{\alpha}\big]|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}.

Then by the fact that
\|\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,s,\cdot,x,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta^{0,2}_{x}(t,s,\cdot,x,y)\|_{\alpha}

is uniformly bounded (see
7.3
), we get

\displaystyle\delta\widehat{\Theta}_{x}^{0}(t,s,x,y)

\displaystyle\leqslant K\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-{\lambda|x-\mu|^{2}\over 4(r-s)}}\over(r-s)^{{n+1\over 2}}}\Big\{|\delta\widehat{\Theta}_{x}^{0}(t,r,\mu,y)|+|\delta\Theta_{x}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x}_{1},\mu,y)||\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{y}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\big]|\tilde{x}_{1}-\tilde{x}_{2}|^{\alpha}\Big\}d\mu dr.

It follows that

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1,0}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\leqslant\sqrt{\varepsilon}K\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\big].

By continuing the above arguments, we have

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},0,\alpha,1,2}([T-\varepsilon,T])}

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\sqrt{\varepsilon}K\big[\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}+\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,1}([T-\varepsilon,T])}\big].

Step 3.
Recalling (
7.18
)–(
7.19
),
similar to (
7.44
)–(
7.45
), we have

\displaystyle\delta\Theta_{x}(s,x)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\Big[\delta\tilde{g}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)

\displaystyle\quad+\Theta^{1}_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\delta\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\Theta_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})\Big]d\mu dr,

(7.49)

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\Big[\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)

\displaystyle\quad+\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})\Big]d\mu dr,

(7.50)

where
\tilde{\Xi}(\cdot)
is defined by (
7.20
). For any
x_{1},x_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
, we have

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},x_{1},y)-\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},x_{2},y)

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big[\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x_{1},r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)-\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x_{2},r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big[\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)+\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})\Big]d\mu dr

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x_{2},r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\Big\{\big[\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)

\displaystyle\qquad-\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\big]+\big[\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)

\displaystyle\qquad-\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\big[\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})

\displaystyle\qquad-\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,x_{2}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})\big]\Big\}d\mu dr.

(7.51)

Note that on
[T-\varepsilon,T]
, by
7.3
(or (
7.17
)) and
7.5
we have

\displaystyle\big|[\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1}))]

\displaystyle-[\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2}))]\big|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\big|[\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))]

\displaystyle\qquad-[\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))]\big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\big|[\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1}))]

\displaystyle\qquad-[\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2}))]\big|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\|\delta\theta^{0}_{xy}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\theta^{1}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}}|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,\alpha,0}}|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big|\int_{0}^{1}\theta^{0,2}_{xy}\big(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},l\theta^{1}(r,x_{1})+(1-l)\theta^{2}(r,x_{1})\big)dl[\theta^{1}(r,x_{1})-\theta^{2}(r,x_{1})]

\displaystyle\qquad-\int_{0}^{1}\theta^{0,2}_{xy}\big(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},l\theta^{1}(r,x_{2})+(1-l)\theta^{2}(r,x_{2})\big)dl[\theta^{1}(r,x_{2})-\theta^{2}(r,x_{2})]\Big|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\Big\{\|\delta\theta^{0}_{xy}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\theta^{1}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}}+\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,\alpha,0}}+\|\theta^{0,2}_{xy}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\big[\|\theta^{0,2}_{xyy}(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\theta^{1}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}}+\|\theta^{2}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}})+\|\theta^{0,2}_{xy}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,\alpha,0}}\big]\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big\}|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,\alpha,1}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}}\big]|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}.

(7.52)

By the same arguments as the above, we have

\displaystyle\big|[\theta^{0,1}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))-\theta^{0,2}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1}))]

\displaystyle-[\theta^{0,1}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))-\theta^{0,2}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2}))]\big|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,\alpha,1}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}}\big]|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha},

(7.53)

and

\displaystyle\big|[\theta_{y}^{0,1}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))-\theta_{y}^{0,2}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1}))]

\displaystyle-[\theta_{y}^{0,1}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))-\theta_{y}^{0,2}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2}))]\big|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\big[\|\delta\theta_{y}^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,\alpha,1}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}}\big]|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}.

(7.54)

For any
\varphi(\cdot)\in C^{1,2}
, from (
7.52
) and the fact

\displaystyle\delta\varphi(x_{1})-\delta\varphi(x_{2})

\displaystyle:=\big[\varphi(x_{1},\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1})))-\varphi(x_{1},\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1})))\big]

\displaystyle\quad-\big[\varphi(x_{2},\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2})))-\varphi(x_{2},\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2})))\big]

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\varphi_{\theta^{0}}\big(x_{1},\ell\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))+(1-\ell)\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1}))\big)d\ell

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big\{[\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1}))]

\displaystyle\qquad-[\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2}))]\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{1}\Big[\varphi_{\theta^{0}}\big(x_{1},\ell\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}))+(1-\ell)\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1}))\big)

\displaystyle\quad-\varphi_{\theta^{0}}\big(x_{2},\ell\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))+(1-\ell)\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2}))\big)\Big]d\ell

\displaystyle\quad\times\big[\theta^{0,1}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}))-\theta^{0,2}_{x}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2}))\big],

we have the following estimate:

\displaystyle|\delta\varphi(x_{1})-\delta\varphi(x_{2})|\leqslant K\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,\alpha,1}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,\alpha}}\big]|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}\quad\hbox{on}\quad[T-\varepsilon,T],

(7.55)

where
K
depends on
\|\varphi(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}
.
Recall that

\displaystyle\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1},y)-\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2},y)

\displaystyle\quad=\Big[\tilde{g}^{0}\big(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1}),\theta^{1}_{x}(r,x_{1}),\theta^{0,1}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1})),\theta_{x}^{0,1}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1})),

\displaystyle\qquad\,\theta_{y}^{0,1}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{1}(r,x_{1})),\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1},y)\big)-\tilde{g}^{0}\big(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1}),\theta^{2}_{x}(r,x_{1}),

\displaystyle\qquad\,\theta^{0,2}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1})),\theta_{x}^{0,2}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1})),\theta_{y}^{0,2}(r,x_{1},r,x_{1},\theta^{2}(r,x_{1})),\Theta^{0,2}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1},y)\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\,-\Big[\tilde{g}^{0}\big(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2}),\theta^{1}_{x}(r,x_{2}),\theta^{0,1}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2})),\theta_{x}^{0,1}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2})),

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\theta_{y}^{0,1}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{1}(r,x_{2})),\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2},y)\big)-\tilde{g}^{0}\big(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2}),\theta^{2}_{x}(r,x_{2}),

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\theta^{0,2}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2})),\theta_{x}^{0,2}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2})),\theta_{y}^{0,2}(r,x_{2},r,x_{2},\theta^{2}(r,x_{2})),\Theta^{0,2}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2},y)\big)\Big].

Using (
7.55
), by the estimates (
7.52
)–(
7.54
),
we get

\displaystyle|\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1},y)-\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{2},y)|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}\big]|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}.

(7.56)

Similarly,

\displaystyle|\delta\tilde{b}(r,x_{1})-\delta\tilde{b}(r,x_{2})|\leqslant K\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big]|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\alpha}.

(7.57)

By (
7.21
),
7.2
and
7.5
, from (
7.51
) we obtain

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leqslant\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}}\Big[|\delta\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)|

\displaystyle\quad+|\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)|+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)|\Big]d\mu dr

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{K\over\sqrt{r-s}}e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}}\Big[|\delta\tilde{b}(r,x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)|+\|\delta\tilde{b}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad+|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x_{1}-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,y)|+\|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}\Big]d\mu dr.

Substituting the estimates (
7.40
), (
7.42
), (
7.56
) and (
7.57
) into the above,
and then by Grönwall’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}\leqslant K\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big].

(7.58)

By the same arguments as the above, we have

\|\delta\Theta_{x}(s,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}\leqslant K\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big].

By continuing the above arguments, we get

\|\delta\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1+\alpha,2}}\leqslant K\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big].

Step 4.

For any
T-\varepsilon\leqslant s_{1}\leqslant s_{2}\leqslant T
, by (
7.50
) we have

\displaystyle\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s_{1},\tilde{x},x,y)-\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,s_{2},\tilde{x},x,y)

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{1},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu)\Big[\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)+\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times\delta\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu)+\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})\Big]d\mu dr

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{s_{2}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big[\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{1},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu)-\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{2},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\mu)\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times\Big[\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)+\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})\Big]d\mu dr

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{s_{2}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{2},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\mu)\Big\{\big[\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)-\delta\tilde{g}^{0}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\mu,y)\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\big[\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)\delta\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu)-\Theta^{0,1}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\mu,y)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times\delta\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\mu)\big]+\big[\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad-\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\mu,y)\tilde{b}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{2}}\mu;\theta^{2},\theta^{0,2})\big]\Big\}d\mu dr

\displaystyle\quad=:(I)+(II)+(III).

(7.59)

By
7.2
and (
7.21
), we get

|(I)|\leqslant\sqrt{s_{2}-s_{1}}K\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,1}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,0,1}}\big].

(7.60)

By
7.2
and (
7.20
)–(
7.21
), we get

\displaystyle|(II)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\Big[\Big|\int_{s_{2}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big[\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{1},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu)-\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s_{2},x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s_{1}}\mu)\big]d\mu dr\Big|+|s_{2}-s_{1}|^{\alpha\over 2}\sqrt{T-s_{1}}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,1}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,0}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leqslant K\Big[\int_{s_{2}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}\over 4}\Big|{1\over\sqrt{r-s_{1}}}-{1\over\sqrt{r-s_{2}}}\Big|d\mu dr+|s_{2}-s_{1}|^{\alpha\over 2}\sqrt{T-s_{1}}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,1}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,0}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leqslant K\big[|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{1\over 2}+|s_{2}-s_{1}|^{\alpha\over 2}\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big]\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,1}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1,0}}\big].

(7.61)

By the estimates (
7.21
), (
7.56
) and (
7.57
), we get

\displaystyle|(III)|\leqslant K\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big]|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}.

(7.62)

Combining the estimates (
7.60
)–(
7.62
) together, we have

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}\leqslant K\varepsilon^{1-\alpha\over 2}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big].

Then it follows from (
7.58
) that

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,\cdot,\tilde{x},x,y)\|_{\alpha\over 2}\leqslant K\varepsilon^{1-\alpha\over 2}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big].

With (
7.49
), by the same arguments as the above, we have

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta_{x}(\cdot,x)\|_{\alpha\over 2}\leqslant K\varepsilon^{1-\alpha\over 2}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big].

By continuing the above arguments, we get

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},1}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,{\alpha\over 2},0,1,2}}\leqslant K\varepsilon^{1-\alpha\over 2}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big].

Step 5.
Combining the estimates in Steps 1–4 together, we get

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},1}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}\leqslant K\varepsilon^{1-\alpha\over 2}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big].

Then by choosing an
0<\bar{\varepsilon}\leqslant\varepsilon
small enough, we get that on
[T-\bar{\varepsilon},T]
,

\displaystyle\|\delta\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},1}}+\|\delta\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{{\alpha\over 2},{\alpha\over 2},\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}\leqslant{1\over 2}\big[\|\delta\theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}+\|\delta\theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}\big].

Thus, (
7.36
) holds and this completes the proof.
∎

Complete the proof of
Theorem 2.10
.

By a routine argument, we can prove that equilibrium HJB equation (
2.25
) admits a unique classical solution

(\Theta(\cdot),\Theta^{0}(\cdot))
on
[T-\varepsilon,T]
, where
\varepsilon
is given by
7.5
.
Thus, to extend the solution to the whole time interval
[0,T]
,
it suffices to prove a global prior estimate for
\|\Theta(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1+\alpha}}
and
\|\Theta^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,1+\alpha,0}}
.

By (
7.26
) and (
7.27
), we get

\displaystyle\|\Theta_{x}(t,s,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta_{x}(s,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leqslant K\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{T}{2K\over\sqrt{r-s}}\big[\|\Theta^{0}_{x}(t,r,\tilde{x},\cdot,y)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}\big]dr.

Let
{\cal M}(\cdot)
be the unique solution of the following integral equation

\displaystyle{\cal M}(s)=K\big[1+\|h(\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|h^{0}(\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,1+\alpha,0}}\big]+\int_{s}^{T}{2K\over\sqrt{r-s}}{\cal M}(r)dr,

and define

\widehat{\cal M}\triangleq\sup_{s\in[0,T]}{\cal M}(s).

Then by the comparison theorem for Volterra integral equations (see
[
68
]
, for example), we get

\|\Theta_{x}(s,\cdot)\|_{C^{\alpha}}+\|\Theta^{0}_{x}(\cdot,s,\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,0,0,\alpha,0}}\leqslant{\cal M}(s)\leqslant\widehat{\cal M}\quad\hbox{on}\quad[0,T].

This completes the proof.

7.2
Proof of
6.3

We first establish a priori estimate for the second-order derivative terms
\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(\cdot)
.
Recalling from (
6.5
), similar to (
7.18
), we have

\displaystyle\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(s,x)

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}(s,x,t+\varepsilon,x-\sqrt{t+\varepsilon-s}\mu;u)\Big[\Theta_{x}(t+\varepsilon,x-\sqrt{t+\varepsilon-s}\mu)

\displaystyle\quad-\tilde{\rho}(s,x,t+\varepsilon,x-\sqrt{t+\varepsilon-s}\mu;u)\Theta(t+\varepsilon,x-\sqrt{t+\varepsilon-s}\mu)\Big]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{t+\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;u)\Big[\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)b(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)

\displaystyle\quad+g\big(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u,\Theta^{\varepsilon}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu),\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\sigma(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)\big)\Big]d\mu dr,

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Xi}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;u):={1\over(4\pi)^{n\over 2}(\hbox{det\,}[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)])^{1\over 2}}e^{-{\langle a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)^{-1}\mu,\mu\rangle\over 4}},

\displaystyle\tilde{\rho}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;u)

\displaystyle\quad:={(\hbox{det\,}[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)])_{x}\over 2\hbox{det\,}[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)]}+{\langle[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)^{-1}]_{x}\mu,\mu\rangle\over 4},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;u)

\displaystyle\quad:={-1\over(4\pi)^{n\over 2}(\hbox{det\,}[a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)])^{1\over 2}}e^{-{\langle a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)^{-1}\mu,\mu\rangle\over 4}}{a(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)^{-1}\over 2\sqrt{r-s}}\mu.

Note that the functions
\tilde{\Xi}(\cdot)
and
\tilde{\rho}(\cdot)
depend on the variable
x
only through the function
a(\cdot)
.
Recall that
a(\cdot)
is a smooth function with bounded derivatives and it satisfies the non-degenerate condition (
6.13
).
Thus, it will not cause any difficulties in the proof.
For the ease of presentation, we assume that

a(s,x,u)=a(s,u),\quad(s,x,u)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n}\times U.

Then by some straightforward calculations, it is easy to obtain that

\displaystyle\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(s,x)

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}(s,x,t+\varepsilon,x-\sqrt{t+\varepsilon-s}\mu;u)\Big[\Theta_{xx}(t+\varepsilon,x-\sqrt{t+\varepsilon-s}\mu)

\displaystyle\qquad-\tilde{\rho}(s,x,t+\varepsilon,x-\sqrt{t+\varepsilon-s}\mu;u)\Theta_{x}(t+\varepsilon,x-\sqrt{t+\varepsilon-s}\mu)\Big]d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{s}^{t+\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\tilde{\Xi}_{x}(s,x,r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu;u)\Big[\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)b_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)b(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)+g_{\theta}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)

\displaystyle\qquad+g_{p}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)+g_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)\Big]d\mu dr,

(7.63)

where
g(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u)\equiv g(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u,\Theta^{\varepsilon}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu),\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)\sigma(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu,u))
.
It follows that

\displaystyle|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(s,x)|

\displaystyle\leqslant K\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}\over 4}\|\Theta(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{C^{2}}d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{t+\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}\over 4}\over\sqrt{r-\mu}}\Big[|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)|+|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(r,x-\sqrt{r-s}\mu)|+1\Big]d\mu dr.

Then by Grönwall’s inequality, we get

\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leqslant K\big[1+\|\Theta(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{C^{2}}\big].

(7.64)

By
7.2
, we also have

\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{0,1}}\leqslant K\big[1+\|\Theta(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{C^{1}}\big].

(7.65)

Thus, similar to (
7.26
), using (
7.63
) and the estimates (
7.64
)–(
7.65
), we have

\displaystyle\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(s,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leqslant K\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}\over 4}\|\Theta(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{C^{2+\alpha}}d\mu

\displaystyle\quad+K\int_{s}^{t+\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}\over 4}\over\sqrt{r-s}}\Big[\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+1\Big]d\mu dr.

Then by Grönwall’s inequality again, we have

\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{C^{\alpha}}\leqslant K\big[1+\|\Theta(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{C^{2+\alpha}}\big].

(7.66)

For any
t\leqslant s_{1}\leqslant s_{2}\leqslant t+\varepsilon
, by (
7.63
) and (
7.66
) we have

\displaystyle|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(s_{1},x)-\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(s_{2},x)|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}\over 4}\big[\|\Theta_{xx}(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta_{x}(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}\big]|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}|\mu|^{\alpha\over 2}d\mu

\displaystyle\qquad+K\int_{s_{1}}^{t+\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}\over 4}\over\sqrt{r-s_{1}}}\big[\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+\|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{xx}(r,\cdot)\|_{\alpha}+1\big]|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}|\mu|^{\alpha\over 2}d\mu dr

\displaystyle\qquad+K\sqrt{s_{2}-s_{1}}+K\int_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}{e^{-\lambda|\mu|^{2}\over 4}\over\sqrt{r-s_{1}}}d\mu dr

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\big[1+\|\Theta(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{C^{2+\alpha}}\big]|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}.

(7.67)

Similar to (
7.33
), we have that for any
t\leqslant s_{1}\leqslant s_{2}\leqslant t+\varepsilon
,

\displaystyle|\Theta^{\varepsilon}(s_{1},x)-\Theta^{\varepsilon}(s_{2},x)|+|\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(s_{1},x)-\Theta^{\varepsilon}_{x}(s_{2},x)|\leqslant K\big[1+\|\Theta(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}\big]|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}.

(7.68)

Moreover, similar to (
7.34
), we have that for any
t\leqslant r\leqslant s_{1}\leqslant s_{2}\leqslant t+\varepsilon
,

\displaystyle|\Theta^{0,\varepsilon}(r,s_{1},\tilde{x},x,y)-\Theta^{0,\varepsilon}(r,s_{2},\tilde{x},x,y)|+|\Theta_{x}^{0,\varepsilon}(r,s_{1},\tilde{x},x,y)-\Theta_{x}^{0,\varepsilon}(r,s_{2},\tilde{x},x,y)|

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant K\big[1+\|\Theta(t+\varepsilon,\cdot)\|_{C^{1+\alpha}}+\|\Theta^{0}(r,t+\varepsilon,\cdot\,,\cdot\,,\cdot)\|_{C^{\alpha,1+\alpha,2}}\big]|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha\over 2}.

(7.69)

Note that
\Theta^{\varepsilon}(t+\varepsilon,x)=\Theta(t+\varepsilon,x)
,
\Theta^{0,\varepsilon}(r,t+\varepsilon,\tilde{x},x,y)=\Theta^{0}(r,t+\varepsilon,\tilde{x},x,y)
,
and that the functions
\Theta(\cdot)
and
\Theta^{0}(\cdot)
are smooth with bounded derivatives.
Then the desired results are obtained from (
7.67
)–(
7.69
).
